PREFACE 


In the preparation of this edition I have used the following 
MSS.: 
S=a paper MS. in Sinhalese character, belonging to 
Professor Rhys Davids. 

M = Mandalay palm-leaf MS. in Burmese character, from 

the India Offce Library. 

I have also consulted the Siamese edition of the 
Patisambhidā and occasionally noted its readings, dis- 
tinguishing it as K. 

In the Preface to my edition of the Katha Vatthu I 
pointed out that the text of that treatise in the Siamese 
edition adhered very closely to the Burmese tradition. 
However, the late Professor Hardy of Wurzburg, in the 
last volume of his edition of the Anguttara-Nikaya, gave it 
as his opinion that, as far as that work was concerned, the 
Siamese text held an intermediate place between the 
Sinhalese and Burmese versions. This statement holds 
good of the Patisambhidā also, where Sinhalese MSS. 
seem to have been utilized to as great an extent as 
Burmese. A curious and instructive instance occurs at 
the end of I. 6 (p. 57 of text). The Mandalay MS. rightly 
reads ‘vedana sañña sankhara tayo khandhā phassasamu- 
daya,” but K. has ‘ vedanā sañña sankhārā ti sese khandha 
phassasamudaya.’ This reading is, of course, impossible, 
but the key to its existence is given by the Sinhalese MS., 
which reads tisso for tayo, and it would seem that the 
Siamese editor, noticing that the gender was wrong and 
apparently having no Burmese MS. to correct him, 
assumed the existence of a break between the two ‘s’ s of 
tisso, thus converting sso into sesā—one has only to write 

v 


vi 


the word in Sinhalese characters to see how easily this 
might happen, especially if the ‘s’ s were not closely 
joined in the MS. In III. 28 (p. 174, note 8) K’s reading 
nirupakkilesasaūkhātena for Sinhalese nirupadhisankhatena 
is probably a gloss; cf. Childers, s.v. ‘ upadhi.’ 

The first two lines of the Gatha in II. 64 (p. 160) occur 
in the Sutta Nipāta 116 (Vasalasutta) ; the remaining 
couplet in our text is not found there, but as it is in 
substance merely the inversion of the preceding lines, its 
addition required no great original effort on the part of the 
compiler of the Patisambhidā. Again, the last line of the 
Gatha on p. 172=the last line of Dhammapada, 178 
(Lokavagga). The whole Patisambhidā Gatha is followed 
in the text by a commentary, and it will be interesting, 
when the Commentary on the Dhammapada is published, 
to see whether Buddhaghosa made any use of this early 
Pitaka exegesis for his own purposes. 

It may be noted here that the Matika gives the contents, 
not of the whole work, but of the Nanakatha only, the 
opening chapter of the Mahavaggo. 

In conclusion, my best thanks are due to the Librarian 
of the India Office for the loan of the Mandalay MS., and 
to Professor Rhys Davids for that of the Sinhalese M8. 
from his collection. 


AnNoLD C. TAYLOR. 
July, 1905.

chattā ; nava dhamma pahātabbā, nava tanhāmūlakā ; 
dasa dhamma pahatabba, dasa micchatta.

padhaniyangani; dasa dhamma bhavetabba, dasa kasiņā- 
yatanani.

5. Katihākārehi sankharupekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo 
hoti ? 

Atthahākārehi sankharupekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo 
hoti. 

Puthujjanassa katihākārehi senkharupekkhaya cittassa 
abhinīhāro hoti, sekhassa katihākārehi sankhārupekkhāya 
cittassa abhinīhāro hoti, vītarāgassa katihākārehi san- 
khārupekkhāya cittassa abhinībāro hoti? 


62 


Puthujjanassa dvihakarehi sankharupekkhaya cittassa 
abhiniharo hoti, sekhassa tīhākārehi sankhārupekkhāya 
cittassa abhinīhāro hoti, vītarāgassa tihakarehi sankhār- 
upekkhaya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. 

6. Puthujjanassa katamehi dvīhākārehi sankharupek- 
khàya cittassa abhiniharo hoti? 

Puthujjano saikharupekkham abhinandati và vipassati 
và; puthujjanassa imehi dvihakarehi sat khürupekkhüya 
cittassa abhinihàro hoti. 

Sekhassa katamehi tīhākārehi sankhārupekkhāya cit- 
tassa abhiniharo hoti? 

Sekho sankharupekkham abhinandati và vipassati và pati- 
sankhaya và phalasamapattim samapajjati; sekhassa imehi 
tīhākārehi saikharupekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo hoti. 

Vītarāgassa  katamehi tīhākārehi sankharupekkhaya 
cittassa abhiniharo hoti? 

Vītarāgo saükharupekkham vipassati và, patisankhāya 
va phalasamápattim samāpajjati, tadajjhupekkhitva suñ- 
fiataviharena va animittaviharena va appanihitaviharena 
va viharati; vītarāgassa imehi tihakarehi sarnkhārupek- 
khāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. 

7. Katham puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca sankhār- 
upekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattam hoti? 

Puthujjanassa sankharupekkham abhinandato cittarh 
kilissati, bhāvanāya paripantho! hoti, pativedhassa 
antarayo hoti, ayatim patisandhiyā paccayo hoti; sek- 
hassa pi sankharupekkham abhinandato cittam kilissati, 
bhāvanāya paripantho hoti, uttaripativedhassa antarāyo 
hoti, ayatim patisandhiya paccayo hoti; evara puthuj- 
janassa ca sekhassa ca sankharupekkhaya cittassa abhini- 
haro hoti abhinandanatthena. 

8. Katham puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vitaragassa ca 
sankharupekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo ekattam hoti? 

Puthujjano sankharupekkham aniccato pi dukkhato pi 
anattato pi vipassati, sekbo saikharupekkham aniccato 
pi dukkhato pi anattato pi vipassati, vītarāgo saūkhār- 


1 paripanto, M. 


63 


upekkham aniccato pi dukkhato pi anattato pi vipassati ; 
evam puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vītarāgassa ca san- 
kharupekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo ekattam hoti anupas- 
sanatthena. 

9. Katham puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vītarāgassa ca 
saīīkbārupekkhāya cittassa abhiniharo nanattam hoti? 

Puthujjanassa. saūkhārupekkhā kusala hoti, sekhassa 
sankharupekkha kusala hoti, vitaragassa sankharupekkha 
abyakata hoti; evam puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vitara- 
gassa ca sankhārupekkhāya cittassa abhiniharo nanattam 
hoti kusalābyākatatthena. 

10. Katham puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vītarāgassa 
ca sankhārupekkhāya cittassa abhiniharo nanattam hoti? 

Puthujjanassa saūkhārupekkhā kiūci kale suvidita hoti 
kiūci kale na suvidita hoti, sekhassa sankhārupekkhā 
kiñei kale suviditā hoti kiñei kale na suvidità hoti, vitara- 
gassa sankharupekkha accantam suviditā hoti; evam 
puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vitaragassa ca sankhār- 
upekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo nanattam hoti viditatthena 
ca aviditatthena ca. 

11. Katham puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vitaragassa 
ca sankhārupekkhāya cittassa abhiniharo nanattarh hoti? 

Puthujjano sankharupekkham 1 atittattā vipassati, sekho 
pi sankhārupekkham atittattā vipassati, vītarāgo san- 
khārupekkham atittattā vipassati ; evam puthujjanassa ca 
sekhassa ca vitaragassa ca sankhārupekkhāya cittassa 
abhinīhāro nanattam hoti tittatthena ca atittatthena ca. 

12. Katham puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vītarāgassa 
ca sankharupekkhaya cittassa abhiniharo nānattam hoti? 

Puthujjano sankharupekkham tinnam  saüüojanànaim 
pahanaya sotāpattimaggam patilābhatthāya vipassati, 
sekho sankharupekkham tinnam saūūojanānam pahīnattā 
uttaripatilabhatthaya vipassati, vītarāgo sankhārupekkham 
sabbakilesānan pahīnattā ditthadhammasukhaviharat- 
thāya vipassati ; evam puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vīta- 
rāgassa ca saūkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhiniharo nānattam 
hoti pahīnatthena ca appahīnatthena ca.

Puthujjano pi saükharupekkham abhinandati va vipas-

và patisankhaya và phalasamāpattim samapajjati, vitarago

samüpattim samāpajjati, tadajjhupekkhitva suūūatavi- 
harena và animittavihārena và appanihitaviharena và 
viharati. Evam puthujjanassa ca sekhassa ca vitaragassa 
ca saükharupekkhaya cittassa abhinihàro nānattam hoti 
vihārasamāpattatthena.

sankharupekkha vipassanāvasena uppajjanti.

janti?

saünnayatanasamapattim patilabhatthaya akincaünfiayatana- 
saiifiam  patisahkha santitthana panna sankhārupek- 
khasu ñanam ; ima attha sankharupekkha samathavasena 
uppajjanti.

jānāti, caritaīh jānāti, adhimuttim jānāti, bhabbabhabbe 
satte pajānāti.

8. Katamo ca sattanam anusayo ? 

Sati’ ānusayā : kāmarāgānusayo patighānusayo mānānu- 
sayo  ditthanusayo  vicikiechànusayo bhavarāgānusayo 
avijjānusayo. Yam loke piyarupam sātarūpam, ettha

asatarüpaim, ettha sattānam patighānusayo anuseti. Iti

5. Katama ca sattanam adhimutti? 

Santi satta hinadhimuttika, santi satta paņītādhimuttikā. 
Hinadhimuttika satta hīnādhimuttike satte sevanti bhajanti 
payirupüsanti, paņītādhimuttikā satta panitadhimuttike 
satte sevanti bhajanti payirupasanti; atitam pi addhānam 
hīnādhimuttikā sattā hinadhimuttike satte sevimsu 
bhajirhsu  payirupasizhsu, paņītādhimuttikā satta pani 
tadhimuttike satte sevimsu  bhajimsu payirupasimsu, 
anagatam pi addhanam hinadhimuttika satta hinadhi- 
muttike satte sevissanti bhajissanti payirupasissanti, paņī- 
tādhimuttikā sattà panitadhimuttike satte sevissanti 
bhajissanti payirupāsissanti. Ayam sattanam adhimutti. 

6. Katame satta abhabba ? 

Ye te sattā kammāvaraņena samannāgatā kilesāvara- 
ņena samannāgatā vipākāvaraņena samannāgatā assaddhā * 
acchanditā duppañña abhabbā niyamam okkamitum 
kusalesu dhammesu sammattarh, ime te sattā abhabba. 

7. Katame sattā bhabbā? 

Ye te sattā na kammavaranena samannāgatā na 
kilesāvaraņena samannāgatā na vipākāvaraņena samanna- 
gata saddhà chandikā paūūavanto bhabba niyāmam 
okkamitum kusalesu dhammesu sammaítam, ime te satta 
bhabba. 


Idam Tathāgatassa sattānam āsayānusaye fāņam.

64. Tayo puggala vipannaditthi, tayo puggala sampan- 
naditthi. 

Katame tayo puggalà vipannaditthi ? 

Titthiyo ca titthiyasavako ca yo ca micchàditthiko ; ime 
tayo puggalā vipannaditthi. 

Katame tayo puggalà sampannaditthī ? 

Tathāgato ca Tathāgatasāvako ca yo ca sammāditthiko ; 
ime tayo puggalā sampannaditthī.

akodhano anupanahi amakkhi? suddhatam gato 
sampannaditthi medhàvi tam jañña ‘ariyo’ itīti.! 


65. Tisso vipannaditthiyo, tisso sampannaditthiyo.

vipannaditthi, ‘Eso me atta’ ti vipannaditthi.

86. ‘ Anupassatīti” Katham tam kāyam anupassati? 
Aniecato anupassati no niccato, dukkhato anupassati no 
sukhato, anattato anupassati no attato, nibbindati no 
nandati, virajjati no rajjati, nirodheti no samudeti, pati- 
nissajjati no ādiyati, aniccato anupassanto niceasaññam 
pajahati, dukkhato anupassanto sukhasaüünam  pajahati, 
anattato anupassanto attasanüarm pajahati, nibbindanto 
nandim pajahati, virajjanto ragam pajahati, nirodhento 
samudayam  pajahati, patinissajjanto adanam  pajahati. 
Evam tam kāyam anupassati. 

‘Bhavana’ ti. Catasso bhavana: tattha jatanam dham- 
mānam anativattanatthena bhāvanā, indriyanam ekaras- 
atthena bhāvanā, tadupagaviriyavahanatthena bhāvanā, 
āsevanatthena bhāvanā.
